public class TL extends java.lang.Object {
    static TL inst;

    int[] data;

    TL() {
        // @method <init>()V
        // @declaration a constructor of `TL`, member flags 0x0000
        // recovered from bytecode; presentation is not claimed to compile

        this.data = new int[]{1, 2, 3};
        TL.inst = this;

    }

        // @declaration a constructor of `TL`, member flags 0x0000

        // @method cloned()[I

        // recovered from bytecode; presentation is not claimed to compile
        return (int[]) this.data.clone();
    }

        // @method sum()I
        // @declaration a static method of `TL`, member flags 0x0008
        // recovered from bytecode; presentation is not claimed to compile

    }

    }

        // recovered from bytecode; presentation is not claimed to compile
